# Stage B fast-channel state-family research gate

## Wall that triggered this gate

The source-command Stage 1 clamp authenticated seven independent failures in fast-sodium activation/deactivation and Kv3-like deactivation, so compartment integration and conductance calibration are stopped.

**Why work stopped:** The filed equation-derived gate roots and current-to-gate time conversions preserve some equilibrium endpoints but do not reproduce the complete powered current while activation, inactivation, and recovery states evolve together.

### Failed attempts already counted

- V1 treated source normalized-conductance Boltzmann curves as unpowered gates and was superseded before execution because m^3 and n^4 shifted the measured curves. (research/findings/2026-08-05-v14-stageB-successor-preregistration-audit-CORRECTION.md)
- V2 applied equation-derived roots and conditional current-to-gate time conversions, but seven complete-current source gates failed under the sealed command protocols. (research/findings/2026-08-05-v14-stageB-fast-channel-source-transfer-STRUCTURAL-NO-GO.md)

No further implementation or tuning against this wall is authorized until every open question below is
resolved from primary evidence or explicitly recorded as absent after an external search.

## Unresolved questions

| ID | Kind | Status | Precise question |
|---|---|---|---|
| P1 | parameter | resolved | Which directly reported transition-rate equations or complete-current model parameters reproduce transient sodium activation midpoint, slope, and 10-90 percent rise together under the Ding, Wei, and Zhou SNr command protocol? |
| P2 | parameter | resolved | Which directly reported sodium transition rates reproduce the composite transient-current deactivation tail at minus 40 mV without breaking the accepted inactivation and biexponential recovery endpoints? |
| P3 | parameter | resolved | Which directly reported Kv3-like transition-rate equations reproduce complete-current deactivation tails at minus 60, minus 50, and minus 40 mV while preserving the accepted activation, inactivation, and rise endpoints? |
| W1 | wiring | resolved | Which coupled closed, open, fast-inactivated, slow-recovery, and blocked states are required by primary evidence for the SNr transient sodium current, and which transitions can be omitted without replacing biological channel dynamics with host logic? |
| W2 | wiring | resolved | Which Kv3.1 and Kv3.4 subunit or state composition is supported for SNr IDR-fast, and what coupled activation-inactivation topology is required to represent its measured current tails? |

- Questions: P1, P2, W1

- Search query: Khaliq Raman resurgent sodium 13-state transition rates activation deactivation recovery
- Exact locator: rsg.mod lines 16-38, 87-106, 114-135, and 156-195
- Relevant evidence: Exact 13-state coupled closed/open/inactivated/blocked topology and voltage-dependent rates provide the first unmodified sodium comparator; preparation transfer to juvenile-rat SNr remains unvalidated.

- Catalog intake: `source-43fe19c85f078a9c`; retrievable: `True`

### S2: Labro et al. 2015 Kv3.1b four-state relaxation model and supplementary parameter table

- Questions: P3, W2
- Source kind: `peer-reviewed-primary`; quality: `high`
- Provenance: [https://www.nature.com/articles/ncomms10173](https://www.nature.com/articles/ncomms10173) (accessed 2026-08-05T10:08:52+00:00)
- Search query: Labro Kv3.1 four-state relaxation activation deactivation hooked tail parameters
- Exact locator: Figure 7b-e; Methods Simulations; Supplementary Table 1
- Relevant evidence: Exact four-state sequential activation/relaxation graph, six voltage-dependent rates, and reference parameters define the smallest unmodified Kv3 deactivation comparator; it contains no inactivation state.
- Quality note: Peer-reviewed primary measurements or methods; may resolve a question.
- Catalog intake: `source-19513313bd30261e`; retrievable: `True`

### S3: Desai et al. 2008 mouse Kv3.3 clamp protocols and compact n^3 availability model

- Questions: P3, W2
- Source kind: `peer-reviewed-primary`; quality: `high`
- Provenance: [https://pmc.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/articles/PMC2494927/](https://pmc.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/articles/PMC2494927/) (accessed 2026-08-05T10:09:57+00:00)
- Search query: Desai Kv3.3 activation inactivation recovery deactivation n3 availability model rates
- Exact locator: Experimental Procedures; Figure 3A-E; numerical simulation section
- Relevant evidence: Matched activation, inactivation, recovery, and deactivation assays plus the source-defined n^3*(0.23+0.77p) current and exponential gate rates define the compact unmodified full-gate Kv3 comparator.
- Quality note: Peer-reviewed primary measurements or methods; may resolve a question.
- Catalog intake: `source-329dbd1a90ef86ec`; retrievable: `True`

Do not graft unsupported inactivation transitions onto Labro.", "Retain the compact classical m^4*h definition only as a negative-control baseline."], "sodium": ["Use Milescu Model III as the most complete CNS waveform-fitted topology reference.", "Use the Khaliq/Raman 13-state model as the immediately executable structural baseline with exact rates.", "Use Balbi Nav1.6 as an isoform-specific comparator, not as sole evidence for deactivation."]} Ordinal research priority; condition: Comparison of all located source-backed candidates against the failed Stage B requirement for waveform-level activation, inactivation, recovery, and deactivation.; sources: milescu-2010, khaliq-2003-model, balbi-2017-paper, linkevicius-2026-paper, linkevicius-2026-code, labro-2015-paper, labro-2015-supplement, desai-2008; locator: Synthesis of the exact model and protocol locators in claims na-milescu-model-iii-coverage through kv3-desai-validation-protocols.; review: approved by codex-controller; limitations: This is a research recommendation, not an accepted biological claim. No candidate has yet been ported, run under the sealed Stage B commands, or compared on identical temperature and preparation conditions. No directly downloadable SNr-specific full kinetic definition was located in this bounded search, but that is not proof that none exists.


## Question dispositions

### P1: resolved

No located source reports a parameter set already shown to reproduce the Ding SNr activation midpoint, slope, rise, and deactivation together. Use the unmodified Khaliq/Raman 13-state rates as the first executable cross-preparation transfer and Balbi only as a comparator; Ding remains the promotion authority.

Evidence records: S1, RP1

### P2: resolved

No directly reported SNr transition rates were located for the minus-40-mV composite sodium tail while preserving Ding inactivation and biexponential recovery. Khaliq/Raman supplies explicit coupled deactivation and recovery paths for a prospective unmodified transfer, not an accepted SNr fit.

Evidence records: S1

### P3: resolved

No located model is already validated against all Ding Kv3 endpoints. Run Labro unmodified for activation/deactivation structure and Desai unmodified as the compact full-gate comparator; learned Channelpedia fits remain waveform oracles only.

Evidence records: S2, S3

### W1: resolved

Primary model evidence supports testing coupled closed, open, conventional inactivated, and open-channel-blocked sodium states. It does not establish that the Khaliq/Raman topology or any omission is uniquely required in SNr, so topology transfer must be tested without post-hoc pruning.

Evidence records: S1

### W2: resolved

Ding does not resolve native SNr Kv3.1/Kv3.4 stoichiometry. Labro supports a four-state relaxation path for Kv3.1 deactivation and Desai supports a separate activation-availability Kv3.3 model; no source supports grafting those transitions into one accepted hybrid.

Evidence records: S2, S3


## Gate decision

Proceed with a prospectively sealed, unmodified source-transfer comparison. Sodium arm A is Khaliq/Raman 13-state and arm B is Balbi Nav1.6. Kv3 arm A is Labro four-state activation/deactivation and arm B is Desai n^3 availability. No hybrid topology, conductance fitting, or temperature correction is authorized in the transfer stage.

**Next bounded experiment:** Preregister exact source/code hashes, original temperature conventions, state equations, initial occupancy rules, sealed Ding command waveforms, complete-current estimators, CPU authority, GPU parity tolerance, and independent per-model stop rules; execute the four unmodified comparator arms before any calibration or Stage 2 integration.

Existing evidence gate: `passed`

## Reuse rule

Future work on the same mechanism must retrieve this artifact, cite the relevant question IDs, and
explain what new evidence changes the decision before repeating a failed attempt.
